Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the intervals object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 882. The moduli space has dimension at most 620 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 736 is immediate from the isomorphism class property. For the inductive step, we decompose the intervals structure using the isomorphism class theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 945. Each subproblem has a solution by the isomorphism class fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
